			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 40:12} % *ISAIAH-40-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-40-12}

			Who hath measured the waters in the hollow of his hand, and meted out heaven with the span, and comprehended the dust of the earth in a measure, and weighed the mountains in scales, and the hills in a balance?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 40:13} % *ISAIAH-40-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-40-13}

			Who hath directed the Spirit of the LORD, or being his counsellor hath taught him?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 40:14} % *ISAIAH-40-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-40-14}

			With whom took he counsel, and who instructed him, and taught him in the path of judgment, and taught him knowledge, and shewed to him the way of understanding?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 40:15} % *ISAIAH-40-15
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-40-15}

			Behold, the nations are as a drop of a bucket, and are counted as the small dust of the balance: behold, he taketh up the isles as a very little thing.

	\subsubsection{Isaiah 41} % *ISAIAH-41
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-41}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 41:1} % *ISAIAH-41-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-41-1}

			Keep silence before me, O islands; and let the people renew their strength: let them come near; then let them speak: let us come near together to judgment.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 41:2} % *ISAIAH-41-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-41-2}

			Who raised up the righteous man from the east, called him to his foot, gave the nations before him, and made him rule over kings? he gave them as the dust to his sword, and as driven stubble to his bow.

			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 41:3} % *ISAIAH-41-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-41-3}

			He pursued them, and passed safely; even by the way that he had not gone with his feet.

			% \scriptsize

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 41:4} % *ISAIAH-41-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-41-4}

			Who hath wrought and done it, calling the generations from the beginning? I the LORD, the first, and with the last; I am he.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 41:5} % *ISAIAH-41-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-41-5}

			The isles saw it, and feared; the ends of the earth were afraid, drew near, and came.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 41:6} % *ISAIAH-41-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-41-6}

			They helped every one his neighbour; and every one said to his brother, Be of good courage.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 41:7} % *ISAIAH-41-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-41-7}

			So the carpenter encouraged the goldsmith, and he that smootheth with the hammer him that smote the anvil, saying, It is ready for the sodering: and he fastened it with nails, that it should not be moved.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 41:8} % *ISAIAH-41-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-41-8}

			But thou, Israel, art my servant, Jacob whom I have chosen, the seed of Abraham my friend.

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 41:9} % *ISAIAH-41-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-41-9}

			Thou whom I have taken from the ends of the earth, and called thee from the chief men thereof, and said unto thee, Thou art my servant; I have chosen thee, and not cast thee away.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

	\subsubsection{Isaiah 42} % *ISAIAH-42
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-42}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 42:1} % *ISAIAH-42-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-42-1}

				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Matthew quotes this verse and the next three in identifying Christ as the servant here, at \hyperref[MATTHEW-12-17]{Matthew 12:17--21}.
					}%
			Behold my servant, whom I uphold; mine elect, in whom my soul delighteth; I have put my spirit upon him: he shall bring forth judgment to the Gentiles.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 42:2} % *ISAIAH-42-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-42-2}

			He shall not cry, nor lift up, nor cause his voice to be heard in the street.

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 42:3} % *ISAIAH-42-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-42-3}

			A bruised reed shall he not break, and the smoking flax shall he not quench: he shall bring forth judgment unto truth.

		\paragraph{Isaiah 42:4} % *ISAIAH-42-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-42-4}

			He shall not fail nor be discouraged, till he have set judgment in the earth: and the isles shall wait for his law.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 42:5} % *ISAIAH-42-5
		% % ---

	\subsubsection{Isaiah 43} % *ISAIAH-43
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-43}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 43:1} % *ISAIAH-43-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-43-1}

			But now thus saith the LORD that created thee, O Jacob, and he that formed thee, O Israel, Fear not: for I have redeemed thee, I have called thee by thy name; thou art mine.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 43:2} % *ISAIAH-43-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-43-2}

			When thou passest through the waters, I will be with thee; and through the rivers, they shall not overflow thee: when thou walkest through the fire, thou shalt not be burned; neither shall the flame kindle upon thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 43:3} % *ISAIAH-43-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-43-3}

			For I am the LORD thy God, the Holy One of Israel, thy Saviour: I gave Egypt for thy ransom, Ethiopia and Seba for thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 43:4} % *ISAIAH-43-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-43-4}

			Since thou wast precious in my sight, thou hast been honourable, and I have loved thee: therefore will I give men for thee, and people for thy life.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 43:5} % *ISAIAH-43-5
		% % ---

		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:2} % *ISAIAH-44-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-2}

		% % ---

			For I will pour water upon him that is thirsty, and floods upon the dry ground: I will pour my spirit upon thy seed, and my blessing upon thine offspring:

		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:5} % *ISAIAH-44-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-5}

			One shall say, I am the LORD's; and another shall call himself by the name of Jacob; and another shall subscribe with his hand unto the LORD, and surname himself by the name of Israel.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:6} % *ISAIAH-44-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-6}

			Thus saith the LORD the King of Israel, and his redeemer the LORD of hosts; I am the first, and I am the last; and beside me there is no God.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:7} % *ISAIAH-44-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-7}

			And who, as I, shall call, and shall declare it, and set it in order for me, since I appointed the ancient people? and the things that are coming, and shall come, let them shew unto them.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:8} % *ISAIAH-44-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-8}

			Fear ye not, neither be afraid: have not I told thee from that time, and have declared it? ye are even my witnesses. Is there a God beside me? yea, there is no God; I know not any.

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:9} % *ISAIAH-44-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-9}

			They that make a graven image are all of them vanity; and their delectable things shall not profit; and they are their own witnesses; they see not, nor know; that they may be ashamed.

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:10} % *ISAIAH-44-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-10}

			Who hath formed a god, or molten a graven image that is profitable for nothing?

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:11} % *ISAIAH-44-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-11}

			Behold, all his fellows shall be ashamed: and the workmen, they are of men: let them all be gathered together, let them stand up; yet they shall fear, and they shall be ashamed together.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:12} % *ISAIAH-44-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-12}

			The smith with the tongs both worketh in the coals, and fashioneth it with hammers, and worketh it with the strength of his arms: yea, he is hungry, and his strength faileth: he drinketh no water, and is faint.

				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:13} % *ISAIAH-44-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-13}

			The carpenter stretcheth out his rule; he marketh it out with a line; he fitteth it with planes, and he marketh it out with the compass, and maketh it after the figure of a man, according to the beauty of a man; that it may remain in the house.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:14} % *ISAIAH-44-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-14}

			He heweth him down cedars, and taketh the cypress and the oak, which he strengtheneth for himself among the trees of the forest: he planteth an ash, and the rain doth nourish it.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:15} % *ISAIAH-44-15
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-15}

			Then shall it be for a man to burn: for he will take thereof, and warm himself; yea, he kindleth it, and baketh bread; yea, he maketh a god, and worshippeth it; he maketh it a graven image, and falleth down thereto.

			% \begin{framed}

				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:16} % *ISAIAH-44-16
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-16}

			He burneth part thereof in the fire; with part thereof he eateth flesh; he roasteth roast, and is satisfied: yea, he warmeth himself, and saith, Aha, I am warm, I have seen the fire:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:17} % *ISAIAH-44-17
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-17}

			And the residue thereof he maketh a god, even his graven image: he falleth down unto it, and worshippeth it, and prayeth unto it, and saith, Deliver me; for thou art my god.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:18} % *ISAIAH-44-18
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-18}

			They have not known nor understood: for he hath shut their eyes, that they cannot see; and their hearts, that they cannot understand.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:19} % *ISAIAH-44-19
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-19}

			And none considereth in his heart, neither is there knowledge nor understanding to say, I have burned part of it in the fire; yea, also I have baked bread upon the coals thereof; I have roasted flesh, and eaten it: and shall I make the residue thereof an abomination? shall I fall down to the stock of a tree?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:20} % *ISAIAH-44-20
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-20}

			He feedeth on ashes: a deceived heart hath turned him aside, that he cannot deliver his soul, nor say, Is there not a lie in my right hand?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:21} % *ISAIAH-44-21
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-21}

			Remember these, O Jacob and Israel; for thou art my servant: I have formed thee; thou art my servant: O Israel, thou shalt not be forgotten of me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:22} % *ISAIAH-44-22
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-22}

			I have blotted out, as a thick cloud, thy transgressions, and, as a cloud, thy sins: return unto me; for I have redeemed thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:23} % *ISAIAH-44-23
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-23}

			Sing, O ye heavens; for the LORD hath done it: shout, ye lower parts of the earth: break forth into singing, ye mountains, O forest, and every tree therein: for the LORD hath redeemed Jacob, and glorified himself in Israel.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:24} % *ISAIAH-44-24
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-24}

			Thus saith the LORD, thy redeemer, and he that formed thee from the womb, I am the LORD that maketh all things; that stretcheth forth the heavens alone; that spreadeth abroad the earth by myself;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:25} % *ISAIAH-44-25
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-25}

			That frustrateth the tokens of the liars, and maketh diviners mad; that turneth wise men backward, and maketh their knowledge foolish;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:26} % *ISAIAH-44-26
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-26}

			That confirmeth the word of his servant, and performeth the counsel of his messengers; that saith to Jerusalem, Thou shalt be inhabited; and to the cities of Judah, Ye shall be built, and I will raise up the decayed places thereof:

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:27} % *ISAIAH-44-27
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-27}

			That saith to the deep, Be dry, and I will dry up thy rivers:

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 44:28} % *ISAIAH-44-28
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-44-28}

			That saith of Cyrus, He is my shepherd, and shall perform all my pleasure: even saying to Jerusalem, Thou shalt be built; and to the temple, Thy foundation shall be laid.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 45} % *ISAIAH-45
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-45}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:1} % *ISAIAH-45-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-1}

			Thus saith the LORD to his anointed, to Cyrus, whose right hand I have holden, to subdue nations before him; and I will loose the loins of kings, to open before him the two leaved gates; and the gates shall not be shut;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:2} % *ISAIAH-45-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-2}

			I will go before thee, and make the crooked places straight: I will break in pieces the gates of brass, and cut in sunder the bars of iron:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:3} % *ISAIAH-45-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-3}

			And I will give thee the treasures of darkness, and hidden riches of secret places, that thou mayest know that I, the LORD, which call thee by thy name, am the God of Israel.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:4} % *ISAIAH-45-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-4}

			For Jacob my servant's sake, and Israel mine elect, I have even called thee by thy name: I have surnamed thee, though thou hast not known me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:5} % *ISAIAH-45-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-5}

			I am the LORD, and there is none else, there is no God beside me: I girded thee, though thou hast not known me:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:6} % *ISAIAH-45-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-6}

			That they may know from the rising of the sun, and from the west, that there is none beside me. I am the LORD, and there is none else.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:7} % *ISAIAH-45-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-7}

			I form the light, and create darkness: I make peace, and create evil: I the LORD do all these things.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:8} % *ISAIAH-45-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-8}

			Drop down, ye heavens, from above, and let the skies pour down righteousness: let the earth open, and let them bring forth salvation, and let righteousness spring up together; I the LORD have created it.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:9} % *ISAIAH-45-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-9}

			Woe unto him that striveth with his Maker! Let the potsherd strive with the potsherds of the earth. Shall the clay say to him that fashioneth it, What makest thou? or thy work, He hath no hands?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:10} % *ISAIAH-45-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-10}

			Woe unto him that saith unto his father, What begettest thou? or to the woman, What hast thou brought forth?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:11} % *ISAIAH-45-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-11}

			Thus saith the LORD, the Holy One of Israel, and his Maker, Ask me of things to come concerning my sons, and concerning the work of my hands command ye me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:12} % *ISAIAH-45-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-12}

			I have made the earth, and created man upon it: I, even my hands, have stretched out the heavens, and all their host have I commanded.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:13} % *ISAIAH-45-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-13}

			I have raised him up in righteousness, and I will direct all his ways: he shall build my city, and he shall let go my captives, not for price nor reward, saith the LORD of hosts.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:14} % *ISAIAH-45-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-14}

			Thus saith the LORD, The labour of Egypt, and merchandise of Ethiopia and of the Sabeans, men of stature, shall come over unto thee, and they shall be thine: they shall come after thee; in chains they shall come over, and they shall fall down unto thee, they shall make supplication unto thee, saying, Surely God is in thee; and there is none else, there is no God.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:15} % *ISAIAH-45-15
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-15}

			Verily thou art a God that hidest thyself, O God of Israel, the Saviour.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:16} % *ISAIAH-45-16
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-16}

			They shall be ashamed, and also confounded, all of them: they shall go to confusion together that are makers of idols.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:17} % *ISAIAH-45-17
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-17}

			But Israel shall be saved in the LORD with an everlasting salvation: ye shall not be ashamed nor confounded world without end.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:18} % *ISAIAH-45-18
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-18}

			For thus saith the LORD that created the heavens; God himself that formed the earth and made it; he hath established it, he created it not in vain, he formed it to be inhabited: I am the LORD; and there is none else.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:19} % *ISAIAH-45-19
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-19}

			I have not spoken in secret, in a dark place of the earth: I said not unto the seed of Jacob, Seek ye me in vain: I the LORD speak righteousness, I declare things that are right.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:20} % *ISAIAH-45-20
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-20}

			Assemble yourselves and come; draw near together, ye that are escaped of the nations: they have no knowledge that set up the wood of their graven image, and pray unto a god that cannot save.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:21} % *ISAIAH-45-21
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-21}

			Tell ye, and bring them near; yea, let them take counsel together: who hath declared this from ancient time? who hath told it from that time? have not I the LORD? and there is no God else beside me; a just God and a Saviour; there is none beside me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:22} % *ISAIAH-45-22
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-22}

			Look unto me, and be ye saved, all the ends of the earth: for I am God, and there is none else.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 45:23} % *ISAIAH-45-23
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-23}

			I have sworn by myself, the word is gone out of my mouth in righteousness, and shall not return, That unto me every knee shall bow, every tongue shall swear.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-45-24}

			Surely, shall one say, in the LORD have I righteousness and strength: even to him shall men come; and all that are incensed against him shall be ashamed.

		% % ---

			In the LORD shall all the seed of Israel be justified, and shall glory.

	\subsubsection{Isaiah 46} % *ISAIAH-46
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-46}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:1} % *ISAIAH-46-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-1}

			Bel boweth down, Nebo stoopeth, their idols were upon the beasts, and upon the cattle: your carriages were heavy loaden; they are a burden to the weary beast.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:2} % *ISAIAH-46-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-2}

			They stoop, they bow down together; they could not deliver the burden, but themselves are gone into captivity.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:3} % *ISAIAH-46-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-3}

			Hearken unto me, O house of Jacob, and all the remnant of the house of Israel, which are borne by me from the belly, which are carried from the womb:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:4} % *ISAIAH-46-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-4}

			And even to your old age I am he; and even to hoar hairs will I carry you: I have made, and I will bear; even I will carry, and will deliver you.

			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:5} % *ISAIAH-46-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-5}

			To whom will ye liken me, and make me equal, and compare me, that we may be like?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:6} % *ISAIAH-46-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-6}

			They lavish gold out of the bag, and weigh silver in the balance, and hire a goldsmith; and he maketh it a god: they fall down, yea, they worship.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:7} % *ISAIAH-46-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-7}

			They bear him upon the shoulder, they carry him, and set him in his place, and he standeth; from his place shall he not remove: yea, one shall cry unto him, yet can he not answer, nor save him out of his trouble.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:8} % *ISAIAH-46-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-8}

			Remember this, and shew yourselves men: bring it again to mind, O ye transgressors.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:9} % *ISAIAH-46-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-9}

			Remember the former things of old: for I am God, and there is none else; I am God, and there is none like me,

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:10} % *ISAIAH-46-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-10}

			Declaring the end from the beginning, and from ancient times the things that are not yet done, saying, My counsel shall stand, and I will do all my pleasure:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:11} % *ISAIAH-46-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-11}

			Calling a ravenous bird from the east, the man that executeth my counsel from a far country: yea, I have spoken it, I will also bring it to pass; I have purposed it, I will also do it.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:12} % *ISAIAH-46-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-12}

			Hearken unto me, ye stouthearted, that are far from righteousness:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 46:13} % *ISAIAH-46-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-46-13}

			I bring near my righteousness; it shall not be far off, and my salvation shall not tarry: and I will place salvation in Zion for Israel my glory.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 47} % *ISAIAH-47
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-47}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:1} % *ISAIAH-47-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-1}

			Come down, and sit in the dust, O virgin daughter of Babylon, sit on the ground: there is no throne, O daughter of the Chaldeans: for thou shalt no more be called tender and delicate.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:2} % *ISAIAH-47-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-2}

			Take the millstones, and grind meal: uncover thy locks, make bare the leg, uncover the thigh, pass over the rivers.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:3} % *ISAIAH-47-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-3}

			Thy nakedness shall be uncovered, yea, thy shame shall be seen: I will take vengeance, and I will not meet thee as a man.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:4} % *ISAIAH-47-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-4}

			As for our redeemer, the LORD of hosts is his name, the Holy One of Israel.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:5} % *ISAIAH-47-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-5}

			Sit thou silent, and get thee into darkness, O daughter of the Chaldeans: for thou shalt no more be called, The lady of kingdoms.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:6} % *ISAIAH-47-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-6}

			I was wroth with my people, I have polluted mine inheritance, and given them into thine hand: thou didst shew them no mercy; upon the ancient hast thou very heavily laid thy yoke.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:7} % *ISAIAH-47-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-7}

			And thou saidst, I shall be a lady for ever: so that thou didst not lay these things to thy heart, neither didst remember the latter end of it.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:8} % *ISAIAH-47-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-8}

			Therefore hear now this, thou that art given to pleasures, that dwellest carelessly, that sayest in thine heart, I am, and none else beside me; I shall not sit as a widow, neither shall I know the loss of children:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:9} % *ISAIAH-47-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-9}

			But these two things shall come to thee in a moment in one day, the loss of children, and widowhood: they shall come upon thee in their perfection for the multitude of thy sorceries, and for the great abundance of thine enchantments.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:10} % *ISAIAH-47-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-10}

			For thou hast trusted in thy wickedness: thou hast said, None seeth me. Thy wisdom and thy knowledge, it hath perverted thee; and thou hast said in thine heart, I am, and none else beside me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:11} % *ISAIAH-47-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-11}

			Therefore shall evil come upon thee; thou shalt not know from whence it riseth: and mischief shall fall upon thee; thou shalt not be able to put it off: and desolation shall come upon thee suddenly, which thou shalt not know.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:12} % *ISAIAH-47-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-12}

			Stand now with thine enchantments, and with the multitude of thy sorceries, wherein thou hast laboured from thy youth; if so be thou shalt be able to profit, if so be thou mayest prevail.

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:13} % *ISAIAH-47-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-13}

			Thou art wearied in the multitude of thy counsels. Let now the astrologers, the stargazers, the monthly prognosticators, stand up, and save thee from these things that shall come upon thee.

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:14} % *ISAIAH-47-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-14}

			Behold, they shall be as stubble; the fire shall burn them; they shall not deliver themselves from the power of the flame: there shall not be a coal to warm at, nor fire to sit before it.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 47:15} % *ISAIAH-47-15
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-47-15}

			Thus shall they be unto thee with whom thou hast laboured, even thy merchants, from thy youth: they shall wander every one to his quarter; none shall save thee.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 48} % *ISAIAH-48
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-48}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:1} % *ISAIAH-48-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-1}

			Hear ye this, O house of Jacob, which are called by the name of Israel, and are come forth out of the waters of Judah, which swear by the name of the LORD, and make mention of the God of Israel, but not in truth, nor in righteousness.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:2} % *ISAIAH-48-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-2}

			For they call themselves of the holy city, and stay themselves upon the God of Israel; The LORD of hosts is his name.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:3} % *ISAIAH-48-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-3}

			I have declared the former things from the beginning; and they went forth out of my mouth, and I shewed them; I did them suddenly, and they came to pass.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:4} % *ISAIAH-48-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-4}

			Because I knew that thou art obstinate, and thy neck is an iron sinew, and thy brow brass;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:5} % *ISAIAH-48-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-5}

			I have even from the beginning declared it to thee; before it came to pass I shewed it thee: lest thou shouldest say, Mine idol hath done them, and my graven image, and my molten image, hath commanded them.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:6} % *ISAIAH-48-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-6}

			Thou hast heard, see all this; and will not ye declare it? I have shewed thee new things from this time, even hidden things, and thou didst not know them.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:7} % *ISAIAH-48-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-7}

			They are created now, and not from the beginning; even before the day when thou heardest them not; lest thou shouldest say, Behold, I knew them.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:8} % *ISAIAH-48-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-8}

			Yea, thou heardest not; yea, thou knewest not; yea, from that time that thine ear was not opened: for I knew that thou wouldest deal very treacherously, and wast called a transgressor from the womb.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:9} % *ISAIAH-48-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-9}

			For my name's sake will I defer mine anger, and for my praise will I refrain for thee, that I cut thee not off.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:10} % *ISAIAH-48-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-10}

			Behold, I have refined thee, but not with silver; I have chosen thee in the furnace of affliction.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:11} % *ISAIAH-48-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-11}

			For mine own sake, even for mine own sake, will I do it: for how should my name be polluted? and I will not give my glory unto another.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:12} % *ISAIAH-48-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-12}

			Hearken unto me, O Jacob and Israel, my called; I am he; I am the first, I also am the last.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:13} % *ISAIAH-48-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-13}

			Mine hand also hath laid the foundation of the earth, and my right hand hath spanned the heavens: when I call unto them, they stand up together.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:14} % *ISAIAH-48-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-14}

			All ye, assemble yourselves, and hear; which among them hath declared these things? The LORD hath loved him: he will do his pleasure on Babylon, and his arm shall be on the Chaldeans.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:15} % *ISAIAH-48-15
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-15}

			I, even I, have spoken; yea, I have called him: I have brought him, and he shall make his way prosperous.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:16} % *ISAIAH-48-16
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-16}

			Come ye near unto me, hear ye this; I have not spoken in secret from the beginning; from the time that it was, there am I: and now the Lord GOD, and his Spirit, hath sent me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:17} % *ISAIAH-48-17
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-17}

			Thus saith the LORD, thy Redeemer, the Holy One of Israel; I am the LORD thy God which teacheth thee to profit, which leadeth thee by the way that thou shouldest go.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:18} % *ISAIAH-48-18
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-18}

			O that thou hadst hearkened to my commandments! then had thy peace been as a river, and thy righteousness as the waves of the sea:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:19} % *ISAIAH-48-19
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-19}

			Thy seed also had been as the sand, and the offspring of thy bowels like the gravel thereof; his name should not have been cut off nor destroyed from before me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:20} % *ISAIAH-48-20
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-20}

			Go ye forth of Babylon, flee ye from the Chaldeans, with a voice of singing declare ye, tell this, utter it even to the end of the earth; say ye, The LORD hath redeemed his servant Jacob.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:21} % *ISAIAH-48-21
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-21}

			And they thirsted not when he led them through the deserts: he caused the waters to flow out of the rock for them: he clave the rock also, and the waters gushed out.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 48:22} % *ISAIAH-48-22
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-48-22}

			There is no peace, saith the LORD, unto the wicked.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 49} % *ISAIAH-49
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-49}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:1} % *ISAIAH-49-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-1}

			Listen, O isles, unto me; and hearken, ye people, from far; The LORD hath called me from the womb; from the bowels of my mother hath he made mention of my name.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:2} % *ISAIAH-49-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-2}

			And he hath made my mouth like a sharp sword; in the shadow of his hand hath he hid me, and made me a polished shaft; in his quiver hath he hid me;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:3} % *ISAIAH-49-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-3}

			And said unto me, Thou art my servant, O Israel, in whom I will be glorified.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:4} % *ISAIAH-49-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-4}

			Then I said, I have laboured in vain, I have spent my strength for nought, and in vain: yet surely my judgment is with the LORD, and my work with my God.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:5} % *ISAIAH-49-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-5}

			And now, saith the LORD that formed me from the womb to be his servant, to bring Jacob again to him, Though Israel be not gathered, yet shall I be glorious in the eyes of the LORD, and my God shall be my strength.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:6} % *ISAIAH-49-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-6}

			And he said, It is a light thing that thou shouldest be my servant to raise up the tribes of Jacob, and to restore the preserved of Israel: I will also give thee for a light to the Gentiles, that thou mayest be my salvation unto the end of the earth.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:7} % *ISAIAH-49-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-7}

			Thus saith the LORD, the Redeemer of Israel, and his Holy One, to him whom man despiseth, to him whom the nation abhorreth, to a servant of rulers, Kings shall see and arise, princes also shall worship, because of the LORD that is faithful, and the Holy One of Israel, and he shall choose thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:8} % *ISAIAH-49-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-8}

			Thus saith the LORD, In an acceptable time have I heard thee, and in a day of salvation have I helped thee: and I will preserve thee, and give thee for a covenant of the people, to establish the earth, to cause to inherit the desolate heritages;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:9} % *ISAIAH-49-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-9}

			That thou mayest say to the prisoners, Go forth; to them that are in darkness, Shew yourselves. They shall feed in the ways, and their pastures shall be in all high places.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:10} % *ISAIAH-49-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-10}

			They shall not hunger nor thirst; neither shall the heat nor sun smite them: for he that hath mercy on them shall lead them, even by the springs of water shall he guide them.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:11} % *ISAIAH-49-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-11}

			And I will make all my mountains a way, and my highways shall be exalted.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:12} % *ISAIAH-49-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-12}

			Behold, these shall come from far: and, lo, these from the north and from the west; and these from the land of Sinim.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:13} % *ISAIAH-49-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-13}

			Sing, O heavens; and be joyful, O earth; and break forth into singing, O mountains: for the LORD hath comforted his people, and will have mercy upon his afflicted.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:14} % *ISAIAH-49-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-14}

			But Zion said, The LORD hath forsaken me, and my Lord hath forgotten me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:15} % *ISAIAH-49-15
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-15}

			Can a woman forget her sucking child, that she should not have compassion on the son of her womb? yea, they may forget, yet will I not forget thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:16} % *ISAIAH-49-16
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-16}

			Behold, I have graven thee upon the palms of my hands; thy walls are continually before me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:17} % *ISAIAH-49-17
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-17}

			Thy children shall make haste; thy destroyers and they that made thee waste shall go forth of thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:18} % *ISAIAH-49-18
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-18}

			Lift up thine eyes round about, and behold: all these gather themselves together, and come to thee. As I live, saith the LORD, thou shalt surely clothe thee with them all, as with an ornament, and bind them on thee, as a bride doeth.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:19} % *ISAIAH-49-19
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-19}

			For thy waste and thy desolate places, and the land of thy destruction, shall even now be too narrow by reason of the inhabitants, and they that swallowed thee up shall be far away.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:20} % *ISAIAH-49-20
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-20}

			The children which thou shalt have, after thou hast lost the other, shall say again in thine ears, The place is too strait for me: give place to me that I may dwell.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:21} % *ISAIAH-49-21
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-21}

			Then shalt thou say in thine heart, Who hath begotten me these, seeing I have lost my children, and am desolate, a captive, and removing to and fro? and who hath brought up these? Behold, I was left alone; these, where had they been?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:22} % *ISAIAH-49-22
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-22}

			Thus saith the Lord GOD, Behold, I will lift up mine hand to the Gentiles, and set up my standard to the people: and they shall bring thy sons in their arms, and thy daughters shall be carried upon their shoulders.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:23} % *ISAIAH-49-23
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-23}

			And kings shall be thy nursing fathers, and their queens thy nursing mothers: they shall bow down to thee with their face toward the earth, and lick up the dust of thy feet; and thou shalt know that I am the LORD: for they shall not be ashamed that wait for me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:24} % *ISAIAH-49-24
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-24}

			Shall the prey be taken from the mighty, or the lawful captive delivered?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:25} % *ISAIAH-49-25
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-25}

			But thus saith the LORD, Even the captives of the mighty shall be taken away, and the prey of the terrible shall be delivered: for I will contend with him that contendeth with thee, and I will save thy children.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 49:26} % *ISAIAH-49-26
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-49-26}

			And I will feed them that oppress thee with their own flesh; and they shall be drunken with their own blood, as with sweet wine: and all flesh shall know that I the LORD am thy Saviour and thy Redeemer, the mighty One of Jacob.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 50} % *ISAIAH-50
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-50}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:1} % *ISAIAH-50-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-1}

			Thus saith the LORD, Where is the bill of your mother's divorcement, whom I have put away? or which of my creditors is it to whom I have sold you? Behold, for your iniquities have ye sold yourselves, and for your transgressions is your mother put away.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:2} % *ISAIAH-50-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-2}

			Wherefore, when I came, was there no man? when I called, was there none to answer? Is my hand shortened at all, that it cannot redeem? or have I no power to deliver? behold, at my rebuke I dry up the sea, I make the rivers a wilderness: their fish stinketh, because there is no water, and dieth for thirst.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:3} % *ISAIAH-50-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-3}

			I clothe the heavens with blackness, and I make sackcloth their covering.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:4} % *ISAIAH-50-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-4}

			The Lord GOD hath given me the tongue of the learned, that I should know how to speak a word in season to him that is weary: he wakeneth morning by morning, he wakeneth mine ear to hear as the learned.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:5} % *ISAIAH-50-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-5}

			The Lord GOD hath opened mine ear, and I was not rebellious, neither turned away back.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:6} % *ISAIAH-50-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-6}

			I gave my back to the smiters, and my cheeks to them that plucked off the hair: I hid not my face from shame and spitting.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:7} % *ISAIAH-50-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-7}

			For the Lord GOD will help me; therefore shall I not be confounded: therefore have I set my face like a flint, and I know that I shall not be ashamed.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:8} % *ISAIAH-50-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-8}

			He is near that justifieth me; who will contend with me? let us stand together: who is mine adversary? let him come near to me.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:9} % *ISAIAH-50-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-9}

			Behold, the Lord GOD will help me; who is he that shall condemn me? lo, they all shall wax old as a garment; the moth shall eat them up.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:10} % *ISAIAH-50-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-10}

			Who is among you that feareth the LORD, that obeyeth the voice of his servant, that walketh in darkness, and hath no light? let him trust in the name of the LORD, and stay upon his God.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 50:11} % *ISAIAH-50-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-50-11}

			Behold, all ye that kindle a fire, that compass yourselves about with sparks: walk in the light of your fire, and in the sparks that ye have kindled. This shall ye have of mine hand; ye shall lie down in sorrow.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 51} % *ISAIAH-51
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-51}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:1} % *ISAIAH-51-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-1}

			Hearken to me, ye that follow after righteousness, ye that seek the LORD: look unto the rock whence ye are hewn, and to the hole of the pit whence ye are digged.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:2} % *ISAIAH-51-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-2}

			Look unto Abraham your father, and unto Sarah that bare you: for I called him alone, and blessed him, and increased him.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:3} % *ISAIAH-51-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-3}

			For the LORD shall comfort Zion: he will comfort all her waste places; and he will make her wilderness like Eden, and her desert like the garden of the LORD; joy and gladness shall be found therein, thanksgiving, and the voice of melody.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:4} % *ISAIAH-51-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-4}

			Hearken unto me, my people; and give ear unto me, O my nation: for a law shall proceed from me, and I will make my judgment to rest for a light of the people.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:5} % *ISAIAH-51-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-5}

			My righteousness is near; my salvation is gone forth, and mine arms shall judge the people; the isles shall wait upon me, and on mine arm shall they trust.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:6} % *ISAIAH-51-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-6}

			Lift up your eyes to the heavens, and look upon the earth beneath: for the heavens shall vanish away like smoke, and the earth shall wax old like a garment, and they that dwell therein shall die in like manner: but my salvation shall be for ever, and my righteousness shall not be abolished.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:7} % *ISAIAH-51-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-7}

			Hearken unto me, ye that know righteousness, the people in whose heart is my law; fear ye not the reproach of men, neither be ye afraid of their revilings.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:8} % *ISAIAH-51-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-8}

			For the moth shall eat them up like a garment, and the worm shall eat them like wool: but my righteousness shall be for ever, and my salvation from generation to generation.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:9} % *ISAIAH-51-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-9}

			Awake, awake, put on strength, O arm of the LORD; awake, as in the ancient days, in the generations of old. Art thou not it that hath cut Rahab, and wounded the dragon?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:10} % *ISAIAH-51-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-10}

			Art thou not it which hath dried the sea, the waters of the great deep; that hath made the depths of the sea a way for the ransomed to pass over?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-11}

			Therefore the redeemed of the LORD shall return, and come with singing unto Zion; and everlasting joy shall be upon their head: they shall obtain gladness and joy; and sorrow and mourning shall flee away.

		% % ---

			I, even I, am he that comforteth you: who art thou, that thou shouldest be afraid of a man that shall die, and of the son of man which shall be made as grass;

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-13}

			And forgettest the LORD thy maker, that hath stretched forth the heavens, and laid the foundations of the earth; and hast feared continually every day because of the fury of the oppressor, as if he were ready to destroy? and where is the fury of the oppressor?

		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:14} % *ISAIAH-51-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-14}

			The captive exile hasteneth that he may be loosed, and that he should not die in the pit, nor that his bread should fail.

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-15}

			But I am the LORD thy God, that divided the sea, whose waves roared: The LORD of hosts is his name.

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-16}

			And I have put my words in thy mouth, and I have covered thee in the shadow of mine hand, that I may plant the heavens, and lay the foundations of the earth, and say unto Zion, Thou art my people.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:17} % *ISAIAH-51-17
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-17}

			Awake, awake, stand up, O Jerusalem, which hast drunk at the hand of the LORD the cup of his fury; thou hast drunken the dregs of the cup of trembling, and wrung them out.
