		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:18} % *ISAIAH-51-18
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-18}

			There is none to guide her among all the sons whom she hath brought forth; neither is there any that taketh her by the hand of all the sons that she hath brought up.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:19} % *ISAIAH-51-19
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-19}

			These two things are come unto thee; who shall be sorry for thee? desolation, and destruction, and the famine, and the sword: by whom shall I comfort thee?

		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:20} % *ISAIAH-51-20
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-20}

			Thy sons have fainted, they lie at the head of all the streets, as a wild bull in a net: they are full of the fury of the LORD, the rebuke of thy God.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:21} % *ISAIAH-51-21
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-21}

			Therefore hear now this, thou afflicted, and drunken, but not with wine:

		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:22} % *ISAIAH-51-22
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-22}

			Thus saith thy Lord the LORD, and thy God that pleadeth the cause of his people, Behold, I have taken out of thine hand the cup of trembling, even the dregs of the cup of my fury; thou shalt no more drink it again:

		\paragraph{Isaiah 51:23} % *ISAIAH-51-23
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-51-23}

			But I will put it into the hand of them that afflict thee; which have said to thy soul, Bow down, that we may go over: and thou hast laid thy body as the ground, and as the street, to them that went over.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 52} % *ISAIAH-52
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-52}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:1} % *ISAIAH-52-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-1}

				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC113-7]{DC 113:7--8}.
					}%
			Awake, awake; put on thy strength, O Zion; put on thy beautiful garments, O Jerusalem, the holy city: for henceforth there shall no more come into thee the uncircumcised and the unclean.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:2} % *ISAIAH-52-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-2}

				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC113-9]{DC 113:9--10}.
					}%
			Shake thyself from the dust; arise, and sit down, O Jerusalem: loose thyself from the bands of thy neck, O captive daughter of Zion.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR2]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:3} % *ISAIAH-52-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-3}

			For thus saith the LORD, Ye have sold yourselves for nought; and ye shall be redeemed without money.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:4} % *ISAIAH-52-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-4}

			For thus saith the Lord GOD, My people went down aforetime into Egypt to sojourn there; and the Assyrian oppressed them without cause.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:5} % *ISAIAH-52-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-5}

			Now therefore, what have I here, saith the LORD, that my people is taken away for nought? they that rule over them make them to howl, saith the LORD; and my name continually every day is blasphemed.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:6} % *ISAIAH-52-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-6}

			Therefore my people shall know my name: therefore they shall know in that day that I am he that doth speak: behold, it is I.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:7} % *ISAIAH-52-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-7}

			How beautiful upon the mountains are the feet of him that bringeth good tidings, that publisheth peace; that bringeth good tidings of good, that publisheth salvation; that saith unto Zion, Thy God reigneth!

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:8} % *ISAIAH-52-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-8}

			Thy watchmen shall lift up the voice; with the voice together shall they sing: for they shall see eye to eye, when the LORD shall bring again Zion.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:9} % *ISAIAH-52-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-9}

			Break forth into joy, sing together, ye waste places of Jerusalem: for the LORD hath comforted his people, he hath redeemed Jerusalem.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:10} % *ISAIAH-52-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-10}

			The LORD hath made bare his holy arm in the eyes of all the nations; and all the ends of the earth shall see the salvation of our God.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 52:11} % *ISAIAH-52-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-11}

			Depart ye, depart ye, go ye out from thence, touch no unclean thing; go ye out of the midst of her; be ye clean,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					This ``Be ye clean'' part until the end appears four times in the scriptures---here, then at \hyperref[3NEPHI-20-41]{3 Nephi 20:41}, \hyperref[DC38-42]{DC 38:42}, and \hyperref[DC133-5]{DC 133:5} (though compare also \hyperref[ISAIAH-66-20]{Isaiah 66:20}).
					}
			that bear the vessels of the LORD.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

			For ye shall not go out with haste, nor go by flight: for the LORD will go before you; and the God of Israel will be your rereward.

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-14}

			As many were astonied at thee; his visage was so marred more than any man, and his form more than the sons of men:

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-52-15}

			So shall he sprinkle many nations; the kings shall shut their mouths at him: for that which had not been told them shall they see; and that which they had not heard shall they consider.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 53} % *ISAIAH-53
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-53}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:1} % *ISAIAH-53-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-1}

			Who hath believed our report? and to whom is the arm of the LORD revealed?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:2} % *ISAIAH-53-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-2}

			For he shall grow up before him as a tender plant, and as a root out of a dry ground: he hath no form nor comeliness; and when we shall see him, there is no beauty that we should desire him.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:3} % *ISAIAH-53-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-3}

			He is despised and rejected of men; a man of sorrows, and acquainted with grief: and we hid as it were our faces from him; he was despised, and we esteemed him not.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:4} % *ISAIAH-53-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-4}

			Surely he hath borne%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					\he{nA,sA'}, which has many meanings, incluidng ``to lift, carry,'' ``to take, take away,'' and so on.
					}
			our griefs, and carried our sorrows: yet we did esteem him stricken, smitten of God, and afflicted.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[BHS] \he{'AkeN .hA:lAyenU hU' nA,sA' Umak:'obeynU s:bAlAM wa'a:na.h:nU .ha:+sab:nuhU nAgU`a muk*eh 'E:lohiyM Um:`un*Eh}
					\item[LXX] \gr{οὗτος τὰς ἁµαρτίας ἡµῶν φέρει καὶ περὶ ἡµῶν ὀδυνᾶται, καὶ ἡµεῖς ἐλογισάµεθα αὐτὸν εἶναι ἐν πόνῳ καὶ ἐν πληγῇ καὶ ἐν κακώσει.}
					\item[BSV] vere languores nostros ipse tulit et dolores nostros ipset portavit et nos putavimus eum quasi leprosum et percussum a Deo et humiliatum
					\item[ASV] Surely he hath borne our griefs, and carried our sorrows; yet we did esteem him stricken, smitten of God, and afflicted.
					\item[ESV] Surely he has borne our griefs and carried our sorrows; yet we esteemed him stricken, smitten by God, and afflicted.
					\item[NASB] Surely our griefs He Himself bore, And our sorrows He carried; Yet we ourselves esteemed Him stricken, Smitten of God, and afflicted.
					\item[NIV] Surely he took up our pain and bore our suffering, yet we considered him punished by God, stricken by him, and afflicted.
					\item[NRSV] Surely he has borne our infirmities and carried our diseases; yet we accounted him stricken, struck down by God, and afflicted.
					% \item[AB ]
					\item[NETS] This one bears our sins and suffers pain for us, and we accounted him to be in trouble and calamity and ill-treatment.
					% \item[OTR1]
					% \item[OTR2]
					% \item[OTX*]
					% \item[OTXX*]
					% \item[OTXXX*]
					% \item[OTXXXX*]
					% \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:5} % *ISAIAH-53-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-5}

			But he was wounded for our transgressions, he was bruised for our iniquities: the chastisement of our peace was upon him; and with his stripes we are healed.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:6} % *ISAIAH-53-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-6}

			All we like sheep have gone astray; we have turned every one to his own way; and the LORD hath laid%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{ISAIAH-53-6-NOTE-laid}%
					There is a powerful parallel here between verses 6 and 12; the word used here, \he{p*Aga`}, is the same as the verb used in verse 12 for ``to make intercession.'' The verb here refers to God burdening Christ with sin and atonement, while the word near the end of verse 12 is then Christ assuming that burden and stepping in to plead for us. See \hyperref[DC19]{DC 19}, especially verse \hyperref[DC19-16]{16}.
					}
			on him the iniquity of us all.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:7} % *ISAIAH-53-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-7}

			He was oppressed, and he was afflicted, yet he opened not his mouth: he is brought as a lamb to the slaughter, and as a sheep before her shearers is dumb, so he openeth not his mouth.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:8} % *ISAIAH-53-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-8}

			He was taken from prison and from judgment: and who shall declare his generation? for he was cut off out of the land of the living: for the transgression of my people was he stricken.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:9} % *ISAIAH-53-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-9}

			And he made his grave with the wicked, and with the rich in his death; because he had done no violence, neither was any deceit in his mouth.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:10} % *ISAIAH-53-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-10}

			Yet it pleased the LORD to bruise him; he hath put him to grief: when thou shalt make his soul an offering for sin, he shall see his seed, he shall prolong his days, and the pleasure of the LORD shall prosper in his hand.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:11} % *ISAIAH-53-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-11}

			He shall see of the travail of his soul, and shall be satisfied: by his knowledge shall my righteous servant justify many; for he shall bear their iniquities.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 53:12} % *ISAIAH-53-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-53-12}

			Therefore will I divide him a portion with the great, and he shall divide the spoil with the strong; because he hath poured out his soul unto death: and he was numbered with the transgressors; and he bare the sin of many, and made intercession%
				\footnote{%
					\label{ISAIAH-53-12-NOTE-intercession}%
					See verse 6, and \hyperref[INTERCESSION]{intercession}.
					}
			for the transgressors.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					% \item[BHS] \he{}
					\item[LXX] \gr{διὰ τοῦτο αὐτὸς κληρονοµήσει πολοὺς καὶ τῶν ἰσχυρῶν µεριεῖ σκῦλα, ἀνθ' ὧν παρεδόθη εἰς θάνατον ἡ} \hyperref[PSUCHE]{\grl{ψυχὴ}} \gr{αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἐν τοῖς ἀνόµοις ἐλογίσθη· καὶ αὐτὸς ἁµαρτίας πολῶν ἀνήνεγκεν καὶ διὰ τὰς ἁµαρτίας αὐτῶν παρεδόθη.}
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[NETS]
					% \item[OTR1]
					% \item[OTR2]

					% \item[OTXXXXX*]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 54} % *ISAIAH-54
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-54}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:1} % *ISAIAH-54-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-1}

			Sing, O barren, thou that didst not bear; break forth into singing, and cry aloud, thou that didst not travail with child: for more are the children of the desolate than the children of the married wife, saith the LORD.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:2} % *ISAIAH-54-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-2}

			Enlarge the place of thy tent, and let them stretch forth the curtains of thine habitations: spare not, lengthen thy cords, and strengthen thy stakes;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:3} % *ISAIAH-54-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-3}

			For thou shalt break forth on the right hand and on the left; and thy seed shall inherit the Gentiles, and make the desolate cities to be inhabited.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:4} % *ISAIAH-54-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-4}

			Fear not; for thou shalt not be ashamed: neither be thou confounded; for thou shalt not be put to shame: for thou shalt forget the shame of thy youth, and shalt not remember the reproach of thy widowhood any more.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:5} % *ISAIAH-54-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-5}

			For thy Maker is thine husband; the LORD of hosts is his name; and thy Redeemer the Holy One of Israel; The God of the whole earth shall he be called.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:6} % *ISAIAH-54-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-6}

			For the LORD hath called thee as a woman forsaken and grieved in spirit, and a wife of youth, when thou wast refused, saith thy God.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:7} % *ISAIAH-54-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-7}

			For a small moment have I forsaken thee; but with great mercies will I gather thee.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:8} % *ISAIAH-54-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-8}

			In a little wrath I hid my face from thee for a moment; but with everlasting kindness will I have mercy on thee, saith the LORD thy Redeemer.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:9} % *ISAIAH-54-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-9}

			For this is as the waters of Noah unto me: for as I have sworn that the waters of Noah should no more go over the earth; so have I sworn that I would not be wroth with thee, nor rebuke thee.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:10} % *ISAIAH-54-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-10}

			For the mountains shall depart, and the hills be removed; but my kindness shall not depart from thee, neither shall the covenant of my peace be removed, saith the LORD that hath mercy on thee.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 54:11} % *ISAIAH-54-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-11}

			O thou afflicted, tossed with tempest, and not comforted, behold, I will lay thy stones with fair colours, and lay thy foundations with sapphires.

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-12}

			And I will make thy windows of agates, and thy gates of carbuncles, and all thy borders of pleasant stones.

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-13}

			And all thy children shall be taught of the LORD; and great shall be the peace of thy children.

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-14}

			In righteousness shalt thou be established: thou shalt be far from oppression; for thou shalt not fear: and from terror; for it shall not come near thee.

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-15}

			Behold, they shall surely gather together, but not by me: whosoever shall gather together against thee shall fall for thy sake.

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-16}

			Behold, I have created the smith that bloweth the coals in the fire, and that bringeth forth an instrument for his work; and I have created the waster to destroy.

		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-54-17}

			No weapon that is formed against thee shall prosper; and every tongue that shall rise against thee in judgment thou shalt condemn. This is the heritage of the servants of the LORD, and their righteousness is of me, saith the LORD.

	\subsubsection{Isaiah 55} % *ISAIAH-55
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-55}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:1} % *ISAIAH-55-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-1}

			Ho, every one that thirsteth, come ye to the waters, and he that hath no money; come ye, buy, and eat; yea, come, buy wine and milk without money and without price.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:2} % *ISAIAH-55-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-2}

			Wherefore do ye spend money for that which is not bread? and your labour for that which satisfieth not? hearken diligently unto me, and eat ye that which is good, and let your soul delight itself in fatness.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:3} % *ISAIAH-55-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-3}

			Incline your ear, and come unto me: hear, and your soul shall live; and I will make an everlasting covenant with you, even the sure mercies of David.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:4} % *ISAIAH-55-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-4}

			Behold, I have given him for a witness to the people, a leader and commander to the people.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:5} % *ISAIAH-55-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-5}

			Behold, thou shalt call a nation that thou knowest not, and nations that knew not thee shall run unto thee because of the LORD thy God, and for the Holy One of Israel; for he hath glorified thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:6} % *ISAIAH-55-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-6}

			Seek ye the LORD while he may be found, call ye upon him while he is near:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:7} % *ISAIAH-55-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-7}

			Let the wicked forsake his way, and the unrighteous man his thoughts: and let him return unto the LORD, and he will have mercy upon him; and to our God, for he will abundantly pardon.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:8} % *ISAIAH-55-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-8}

			For my thoughts are not your thoughts, neither are your ways my ways, saith the LORD.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:9} % *ISAIAH-55-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-9}

			For as the heavens are higher than the earth, so are my ways higher than your ways, and my thoughts than your thoughts.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:10} % *ISAIAH-55-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-10}

			For as the rain cometh down, and the snow from heaven, and returneth not thither, but watereth the earth, and maketh it bring forth and bud, that it may give seed to the sower, and bread to the eater:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

				% \end{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:11} % *ISAIAH-55-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-11}

			So shall my word be that goeth forth out of my mouth: it shall not return unto me void, but it shall accomplish that which I please, and it shall prosper in the thing whereto I sent it.

				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:12} % *ISAIAH-55-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-12}

			For ye shall go out with joy, and be led forth with peace: the mountains and the hills shall break forth before you into singing, and all the trees of the field shall clap their hands.

				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 55:13} % *ISAIAH-55-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-55-13}

			Instead of the thorn shall come up the fir tree, and instead of the brier shall come up the myrtle tree: and it shall be to the LORD for a name, for an everlasting sign that shall not be cut off.

	\subsubsection{Isaiah 56} % *ISAIAH-56
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-56}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:1} % *ISAIAH-56-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-1}

			Thus saith the LORD, Keep ye judgment, and do justice: for my salvation is near to come, and my righteousness to be revealed.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:2} % *ISAIAH-56-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-2}

			Blessed is the man that doeth this, and the son of man that layeth hold on it; that keepeth the sabbath from polluting it, and keepeth his hand from doing any evil.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:3} % *ISAIAH-56-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-3}

			Neither let the son of the stranger, that hath joined himself to the LORD, speak, saying, The LORD hath utterly separated me from his people: neither let the eunuch say, Behold, I am a dry tree.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:4} % *ISAIAH-56-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-4}

			For thus saith the LORD unto the eunuchs that keep my sabbaths, and choose the things that please me, and take hold of my covenant;

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:5} % *ISAIAH-56-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-5}

			Even unto them will I give in mine house and within my walls a place and a name better than of sons and of daughters: I will give them an everlasting name, that shall not be cut off.

			% \begin{framed}

				% \begin{compactdesc}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:6} % *ISAIAH-56-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-6}

			Also the sons of the stranger, that join themselves to the LORD, to serve him, and to love the name of the LORD, to be his servants, every one that keepeth the sabbath from polluting it, and taketh hold of my covenant;

			% \begin{framed}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:7} % *ISAIAH-56-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-7}

			Even them will I bring to my holy mountain, and make them joyful in my house of prayer: their burnt offerings and their sacrifices shall be accepted upon mine altar; for mine house shall be called an house of prayer for all people.

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:8} % *ISAIAH-56-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-8}

			The Lord GOD, which gathereth the outcasts of Israel saith, Yet will I gather others to him, beside those that are gathered unto him.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:9} % *ISAIAH-56-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-9}

			All ye beasts of the field, come to devour, yea, all ye beasts in the forest.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:10} % *ISAIAH-56-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-10}

			His watchmen are blind: they are all ignorant, they are all dumb dogs, they cannot bark; sleeping, lying down, loving to slumber.

			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:11} % *ISAIAH-56-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-11}

			Yea, they are greedy dogs which can never have enough, and they are shepherds that cannot understand: they all look to their own way, every one for his gain, from his quarter.

			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 56:12} % *ISAIAH-56-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-56-12}

			Come ye, say they, I will fetch wine, and we will fill ourselves with strong drink; and to morrow shall be as this day, and much more abundant.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 57} % *ISAIAH-57
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-57}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:1} % *ISAIAH-57-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-1}

			The righteous perisheth, and no man layeth it to heart: and merciful men are taken away, none considering that the righteous is taken away from the evil to come.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:2} % *ISAIAH-57-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-2}

			He shall enter into peace: they shall rest in their beds, each one walking in his uprightness.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:3} % *ISAIAH-57-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-3}

			But draw near hither, ye sons of the sorceress, the seed of the adulterer and the whore.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:4} % *ISAIAH-57-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-4}

			Against whom do ye sport yourselves? against whom make ye a wide mouth, and draw out the tongue? are ye not children of transgression, a seed of falsehood,

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:5} % *ISAIAH-57-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-5}

			Enflaming yourselves with idols under every green tree, slaying the children in the valleys under the clifts of the rocks?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:6} % *ISAIAH-57-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-6}

			Among the smooth stones of the stream is thy portion; they, they are thy lot: even to them hast thou poured a drink offering, thou hast offered a meat offering. Should I receive comfort in these?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:7} % *ISAIAH-57-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-7}

			Upon a lofty and high mountain hast thou set thy bed: even thither wentest thou up to offer sacrifice.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:8} % *ISAIAH-57-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-8}

			Behind the doors also and the posts hast thou set up thy remembrance: for thou hast discovered thyself to another than me, and art gone up; thou hast enlarged thy bed, and made thee a covenant with them; thou lovedst their bed where thou sawest it.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:9} % *ISAIAH-57-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-9}

			And thou wentest to the king with ointment, and didst increase thy perfumes, and didst send thy messengers far off, and didst debase thyself even unto hell.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:10} % *ISAIAH-57-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-10}

			Thou art wearied in the greatness of thy way; yet saidst thou not, There is no hope: thou hast found the life of thine hand; therefore thou wast not grieved.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:11} % *ISAIAH-57-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-11}

			And of whom hast thou been afraid or feared, that thou hast lied, and hast not remembered me, nor laid it to thy heart? have not I held my peace even of old, and thou fearest me not?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:12} % *ISAIAH-57-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-12}

			I will declare thy righteousness, and thy works; for they shall not profit thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:13} % *ISAIAH-57-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-13}

			When thou criest, let thy companies deliver thee; but the wind shall carry them all away; vanity shall take them: but he that putteth his trust in me shall possess the land, and shall inherit my holy mountain;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:14} % *ISAIAH-57-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-14}

			And shall say, Cast ye up, cast ye up, prepare the way, take up the stumblingblock out of the way of my people.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:15} % *ISAIAH-57-15
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-15}

			For thus saith the high and lofty One that inhabiteth eternity, whose name is Holy; I dwell in the high and holy place, with him also that is of a \hyperref[CONTRITE]{contrite} and humble spirit, to revive the spirit of the humble, and to revive the heart of the \hyperref[CONTRITE]{contrite} ones.

			% \begin{framed}

				% \begin{compactdesc}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:16} % *ISAIAH-57-16
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-16}

			For I will not contend for ever, neither will I be always wroth: for the spirit should fail before me, and the souls which I have made.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:17} % *ISAIAH-57-17
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-17}

			For the iniquity of his covetousness was I wroth, and smote him: I hid me, and was wroth, and he went on frowardly in the way of his heart.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:18} % *ISAIAH-57-18
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-18}

			I have seen his ways, and will heal him: I will lead him also, and restore comforts unto him and to his mourners.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:19} % *ISAIAH-57-19
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-19}

			I create the fruit of the lips; Peace, peace to him that is far off, and to him that is near, saith the LORD; and I will heal him.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:20} % *ISAIAH-57-20
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-20}

			But the wicked are like the troubled sea, when it cannot rest, whose waters cast up mire and dirt.

			% \begin{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 57:21} % *ISAIAH-57-21
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-57-21}

			There is no peace, saith my God, to the wicked.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 58} % *ISAIAH-58
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-58}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:1} % *ISAIAH-58-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-1}

			Cry aloud, spare not, lift up thy voice like a trumpet, and shew my people their transgression, and the house of Jacob their sins.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:2} % *ISAIAH-58-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-2}

			Yet they seek me daily, and delight to know my ways, as a nation that did righteousness, and forsook not the ordinance of their God: they ask of me the ordinances of justice; they take delight in approaching to God.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:3} % *ISAIAH-58-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-3}

			Wherefore have we fasted, say they, and thou seest not? wherefore have we afflicted our soul, and thou takest no knowledge? Behold, in the day of your fast ye find pleasure, and exact all your labours.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:4} % *ISAIAH-58-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-4}

			Behold, ye fast for strife and debate, and to smite with the fist of wickedness: ye shall not fast as ye do this day, to make your voice to be heard on high.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:5} % *ISAIAH-58-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-5}

			Is it such a fast that I have chosen? a day for a man to afflict his soul? is it to bow down his head as a bulrush, and to spread sackcloth and ashes under him? wilt thou call this a fast, and an acceptable day to the LORD?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:6} % *ISAIAH-58-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-6}

			Is not this the fast that I have chosen? to loose the bands of wickedness, to undo the heavy burdens, and to let the oppressed go free, and that ye break every yoke?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:7} % *ISAIAH-58-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-7}

			Is it not to deal thy bread to the hungry, and that thou bring the poor that are cast out to thy house? when thou seest the naked, that thou cover him; and that thou hide not thyself from thine own flesh?

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:8} % *ISAIAH-58-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-8}

			Then shall thy light break forth as the morning, and thine health shall spring forth speedily: and thy righteousness shall go before thee; the glory of the LORD shall be thy rereward.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:9} % *ISAIAH-58-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-9}

			Then shalt thou call, and the LORD shall answer; thou shalt cry, and he shall say, Here I am. If thou take away from the midst of thee the yoke, the putting forth of the finger, and speaking vanity;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:10} % *ISAIAH-58-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-10}

			And if thou draw out thy soul to the hungry, and satisfy the afflicted soul; then shall thy light rise in obscurity, and thy darkness be as the noon day:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:11} % *ISAIAH-58-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-11}

			And the LORD shall guide thee continually, and satisfy thy soul in drought, and make fat thy bones: and thou shalt be like a watered garden, and like a spring of water, whose waters fail not.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:12} % *ISAIAH-58-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-12}

			And they that shall be of thee shall build the old waste places: thou shalt raise up the foundations of many generations; and thou shalt be called, The repairer of the breach, The restorer of paths to dwell in.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:13} % *ISAIAH-58-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-13}

			If thou turn away thy foot from the sabbath, from doing thy pleasure on my holy day; and call the sabbath a delight, the holy of the LORD, honourable; and shalt honour him, not doing thine own ways, nor finding thine own pleasure, nor speaking thine own words:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 58:14} % *ISAIAH-58-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-58-14}

			Then shalt thou delight thyself in the LORD; and I will cause thee to ride upon the high places of the earth, and feed thee with the heritage of Jacob thy father: for the mouth of the LORD hath spoken it.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 59} % *ISAIAH-59
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-59}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:1} % *ISAIAH-59-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-1}

			Behold, the LORD's hand is not shortened, that it cannot save; neither his ear heavy, that it cannot hear:

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:2} % *ISAIAH-59-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-2}

			But your iniquities have separated between you and your God, and your sins have hid his face from you, that he will not hear.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:3} % *ISAIAH-59-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-3}

			For your hands are defiled with blood, and your fingers with iniquity; your lips have spoken lies, your tongue hath muttered perverseness.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:4} % *ISAIAH-59-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-4}

			None calleth for justice, nor any pleadeth for truth: they trust in vanity, and speak lies; they conceive mischief, and bring forth iniquity.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:5} % *ISAIAH-59-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-5}

			They hatch cockatrice' eggs, and weave the spider's web: he that eateth of their eggs dieth, and that which is crushed breaketh out into a viper.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:6} % *ISAIAH-59-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-6}

			Their webs shall not become garments, neither shall they cover themselves with their works: their works are works of iniquity, and the act of violence is in their hands.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:7} % *ISAIAH-59-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-7}

			Their feet run to evil, and they make haste to shed innocent blood: their thoughts are thoughts of iniquity; wasting and destruction are in their paths.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:8} % *ISAIAH-59-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-8}

			The way of peace they know not; and there is no judgment in their goings: they have made them crooked paths: whosoever goeth therein shall not know peace.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:9} % *ISAIAH-59-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-9}

			Therefore is judgment far from us, neither doth justice overtake us: we wait for light, but behold obscurity; for brightness, but we walk in darkness.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:10} % *ISAIAH-59-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-10}

			We grope for the wall like the blind, and we grope as if we had no eyes: we stumble at noon day as in the night; we are in desolate places as dead men.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:11} % *ISAIAH-59-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-11}

			We roar all like bears, and mourn sore like doves: we look for judgment, but there is none; for salvation, but it is far off from us.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:12} % *ISAIAH-59-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-12}

			For our transgressions are multiplied before thee, and our sins testify against us: for our transgressions are with us; and as for our iniquities, we know them;

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:13} % *ISAIAH-59-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-13}

			In transgressing and lying against the LORD, and departing away from our God, speaking oppression and revolt, conceiving and uttering from the heart words of falsehood.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:14} % *ISAIAH-59-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-14}

			And judgment is turned away backward, and justice standeth afar off: for truth is fallen in the street, and equity cannot enter.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:15} % *ISAIAH-59-15
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-15}

			Yea, truth faileth; and he that departeth from evil maketh himself a prey: and the LORD saw it, and it displeased him that there was no judgment.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:16} % *ISAIAH-59-16
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-16}

			And he saw that there was no man, and wondered that there was no intercessor: therefore his arm brought salvation unto him; and his righteousness, it sustained him.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:17} % *ISAIAH-59-17
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-17}

			For he put on righteousness as a breastplate, and an helmet of salvation upon his head; and he put on the garments of vengeance for clothing, and was clad with zeal as a cloak.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:18} % *ISAIAH-59-18
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-18}

			According to their deeds, accordingly he will repay, fury to his adversaries, recompence to his enemies; to the islands he will repay recompence.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:19} % *ISAIAH-59-19
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-19}

			So shall they fear the name of the LORD from the west, and his glory from the rising of the sun. When the enemy shall come in like a flood, the Spirit of the LORD shall lift up a standard against him.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:20} % *ISAIAH-59-20
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-20}

			And the Redeemer shall come to Zion, and unto them that turn from transgression in Jacob, saith the LORD.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 59:21} % *ISAIAH-59-21
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-59-21}

			As for me, this is my covenant with them, saith the LORD; My spirit that is upon thee, and my words which I have put in thy mouth, shall not depart out of thy mouth, nor out of the mouth of thy seed, nor out of the mouth of thy seed's seed, saith the LORD, from henceforth and for ever.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Isaiah 60} % *ISAIAH-60
	% -----
		\label{ISAIAH-60}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:1} % *ISAIAH-60-1
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-1}

			Arise, shine; for thy light is come, and the glory of the LORD is risen upon thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:2} % *ISAIAH-60-2
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-2}

			For, behold, the darkness shall cover the earth, and gross darkness the people: but the LORD shall arise upon thee, and his glory shall be seen upon thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:3} % *ISAIAH-60-3
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-3}

			And the Gentiles shall come to thy light, and kings to the brightness of thy rising.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:4} % *ISAIAH-60-4
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-4}

			Lift up thine eyes round about, and see: all they gather themselves together, they come to thee: thy sons shall come from far, and thy daughters shall be nursed at thy side.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:5} % *ISAIAH-60-5
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-5}

			Then thou shalt see, and flow together, and thine heart shall fear, and be enlarged; because the abundance of the sea shall be converted unto thee, the forces of the Gentiles shall come unto thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:6} % *ISAIAH-60-6
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-6}

			The multitude of camels shall cover thee, the dromedaries of Midian and Ephah; all they from Sheba shall come: they shall bring gold and incense; and they shall shew forth the praises of the LORD.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:7} % *ISAIAH-60-7
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-7}

			All the flocks of Kedar shall be gathered together unto thee, the rams of Nebaioth shall minister unto thee: they shall come up with acceptance on mine altar, and I will glorify the house of my glory.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[ASV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:8} % *ISAIAH-60-8
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-8}

			Who are these that fly as a cloud, and as the doves to their windows?

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:9} % *ISAIAH-60-9
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-9}

			Surely the isles shall wait for me, and the ships of Tarshish first, to bring thy sons from far, their silver and their gold with them, unto the name of the LORD thy God, and to the Holy One of Israel, because he hath glorified thee.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:10} % *ISAIAH-60-10
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-10}

			And the sons of strangers shall build up thy walls, and their kings shall minister unto thee: for in my wrath I smote thee, but in my favour have I had mercy on thee.

			% \begin{framed}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:11} % *ISAIAH-60-11
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-11}

			Therefore thy gates shall be open continually; they shall not be shut day nor night; that men may bring unto thee the forces of the Gentiles, and that their kings may be brought.

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:12} % *ISAIAH-60-12
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-12}

			For the nation and kingdom that will not serve thee shall perish; yea, those nations shall be utterly wasted.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:13} % *ISAIAH-60-13
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-13}

			The glory of Lebanon shall come unto thee, the fir tree, the pine tree, and the box together, to beautify the place of my sanctuary; and I will make the place of my feet glorious.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:14} % *ISAIAH-60-14
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-14}

			The sons also of them that afflicted thee shall come bending unto thee; and all they that despised thee shall bow themselves down at the soles of thy feet; and they shall call thee, The city of the LORD, The Zion of the Holy One of Israel.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:15} % *ISAIAH-60-15
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-15}

			Whereas thou hast been forsaken and hated, so that no man went through thee, I will make thee an eternal excellency, a joy of many generations.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:16} % *ISAIAH-60-16
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-16}

			Thou shalt also suck the milk of the Gentiles, and shalt suck the breast of kings: and thou shalt know that I the LORD am thy Saviour and thy Redeemer, the mighty One of Jacob.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:17} % *ISAIAH-60-17
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-17}

			For brass I will bring gold, and for iron I will bring silver, and for wood brass, and for stones iron: I will also make thy officers peace, and thine exactors righteousness.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:18} % *ISAIAH-60-18
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-18}

			Violence shall no more be heard in thy land, wasting nor destruction within thy borders; but thou shalt call thy walls Salvation, and thy gates Praise.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:19} % *ISAIAH-60-19
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-19}

			The sun shall be no more thy light by day; neither for brightness shall the moon give light unto thee: but the LORD shall be unto thee an everlasting light, and thy God thy glory.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:20} % *ISAIAH-60-20
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-20}

			Thy sun shall no more go down; neither shall thy moon withdraw itself: for the LORD shall be thine everlasting light, and the days of thy mourning shall be ended.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:21} % *ISAIAH-60-21
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-21}

			Thy people also shall be all righteous: they shall inherit the land for ever, the branch of my planting, the work of my hands, that I may be glorified.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}

				% \end{compactdesc}
			% \end{framed}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Isaiah 60:22} % *ISAIAH-60-22
		% % ---
			\label{ISAIAH-60-22}

			A little one shall become a thousand, and a small one a strong nation: I the LORD will hasten it in his time.

			% \begin{framed}
			% \scriptsize
				% \begin{compactdesc}
					% % \item[BHS] \he{}
